Fix: always reset to `DEFAULT_STATUS` on add/replace. `addSession` is only called after `window.api.login` succeeds, so `connected: true` is correct at that point; the next vitals events fill in the rest. |
| B95 | A character configured for DRT (or DRX or DRF) connects to DR regardless of the saved game | v0.8.0 | Reported by Binu — a brand-new DRT character logged in via Lich landed in DR. Audit found a four-layer chain of dropped game info, each layer reinforcing the next: **(1)** `LoginCredentials` ([shared/types.ts](src/shared/types.ts)) had no `game` field, so the renderer literally couldn't tell main which shard. **(2)** `App.tsx#runConnect` set `lichPort: adv.lichPort` — the *global* last-saved port from `_shared.yaml`, NOT derived from `c.game`. A DRT character (port 11624 by Lich convention) used whatever port the global setting pointed at, almost always 11024 = DR. **(3)** `LichConnection.launch` hardcoded its spawn args to `[lichPath, mode, '--dragonrealms']` — Lich always launched in DR mode, never `--test --dragonrealms` for DRT, never `--platinum --dragonrealms` for DRX, never `--fallen` for DRF. **(4)** `ConnectionManager.authenticateSge` and `connectDirect` called `this.sge.authenticate(creds.account, creds.password)` without the gameCode third arg — `SGEConnection.authenticate` defaulted to `'DR'`, so even the SGE-issued login key was for DR. With all four layers stacked, DRT/DRX/DRF effectively did not work at all. Fix threads `game` end-to-end: added `game` and `lichArguments` to `LoginCredentials`; `runConnect` and the wizard's `finish()` derive port + args via the new `gameOptionByCode()` helper (single source of truth in [lichSettings.ts](src/renderer/lichSettings.ts) `GAMES`, which now carries `lichArguments` alongside the port); `LichConnection.launch` accepts the argument string and splits it into `cp.spawn` args; SGE auth threads `creds.game` through to `sge.authenticate(...)`. A CLAUDE.md pitfall was added so this footgun shape (per-character connection params silently using a global default) doesn't recur. |
| B94 | Fresh-install user without Cascadia Code sees the game font visibly flip the moment they open Settings; the Settings panel header also briefly shows the raw `cascadia` key string | v0.7.1 | Self-found during a v0.7.1 bug-test pass on the B93 font-default change. Root cause: B93 set `DEFAULT_SETTINGS.fontFamily = 'cascadia'` (a key into the `FONT_FAMILIES` chain, deliberately broader than a literal font name so users without Cascadia fall through `Fira Code → Consolas → monospace`). But `LEGACY_KEYS` in [SettingsPanel.tsx](src/renderer/components/SettingsPanel.tsx) still contained `cascadia: 'Cascadia Code'` from a much earlier migration — when the SettingsPanel mounted, that migration fired and rewrote `fontFamily` from `'cascadia'` (broad chain) to `'Cascadia Code'` (narrow `'Cascadia Code', monospace`). On a Win10 machine without Windows Terminal (so no Cascadia bundled), the font visibly changed from Consolas to generic monospace at that moment. The SettingsPanel header also showed the literal `cascadia` string for one render frame, and the font list never highlighted any item (no installed font is literally named "cascadia"). Fix: removed the `cascadia` entry from `LEGACY_KEYS` (the other three — terminal/sansserif/serif — are still truly legacy and stay). Header now renders `FONT_FAMILY_LABELS[fontFamily] ?? fontFamily` so the legacy-key state displays as "Cascadia Code (default)". List highlight resolves the legacy key to the first font in the FONT_FAMILIES chain so the right row lights up. No data migration — existing profiles unaffected. |
| B93 | The whole client "feels too heavy" / extra-bold compared to Genie or Frostbite — regular game text reads as already bold, and hand-held / spell-active states snap from regular straight to extra-bold | v0.7.1 | Reported by Sekmeht and Binu. Two root causes, audit-confirmed: **(1)** Default font was **Consolas**, which only ships with two weights (400 and 700) — every `font-weight: 600` declaration in the codebase (hands, spell, status bars, panel tabs, character tabs, vitals) silently fell back to full-bold 700, collapsing the UI's intended intermediate-emphasis tier. So "soft emphasis" was actually full bold everywhere. **(2)** `[data-preset="bold"]` and `[data-preset="roomname"]` used `font-weight: bold` (700) — a standard CSS bold, but combined with (1) the whole UI sat at either 400 or 700 with no middle ground. The 700 game-bold against an already-heavy-feeling 400 UI read as extra-bold rather than just bold. Fix, three coordinated changes: **(a)** `DEFAULT_SETTINGS.fontFamily` switched from `'Consolas'` to `'cascadia'` (Cascadia Code — bundled with Windows Terminal and stock on Windows 11; already in the CSS fallback chain since v0.1, just not the default). Cascadia ships weights 200–700 including 500 and 600, so intermediate-emphasis declarations actually render at their intended weight. Players who explicitly picked Consolas in their profile keep it (no migration) — only fresh installs / unset characters get the new default. **(b)** `.hand-held .hand-item` and `.spell-active` lost their `font-weight: 600`; held / active states now read via color change (and the existing spell-glow) instead of a weight snap. **(c)** `[data-preset="bold"]` and `[data-preset="roomname"]` dropped from `bold` (700) to `600` (real semibold on Cascadia, falls back to 700 on Consolas — no regression for opt-in Consolas users). Settings preview chips updated to match. Other 600/700 declarations (status bars, vitals, toolbar, character tabs, panel tabs) were inventoried but left as-is — visible as UI chrome, not in the "game text feels bold" complaint surface; can be revisited if testers find them too heavy now that the font default changed. |
| B92 | `Ctrl+Shift+Enter` (Quick-Send) opens with an empty input — anything you'd typed in the active command bar has to be retyped to target another character | v0.7.1 | Reported by the developer. The chord set `showQuickSend(true)` with no payload — QuickSend's `command` state was initialized to `''` regardless of what the player had been composing, so a half-typed `cast 1701` you'd decided belonged on your alt had to be retyped from scratch. Fix: App.tsx snapshots `.command-input`'s value at the moment the chord fires; `showQuickSend` widened from `boolean` to `{ initialCommand: string } \| null` so the value rides through to the modal. QuickSend now accepts `initialCommand` and uses it as initial state; on open it also `select()`s the input so a prefilled value can be replaced mid-typing or sent as-is with Enter. The source bar is intentionally left untouched (less destructive than auto-clearing — Esc-cancel preserves what you were composing). |
| B91 | `Ctrl+#` / `Ctrl+Tab` switches the active character but doesn't move focus to the new tab's command bar — you have to click into it before you can type | v0.7.1 | Reported by Binu. The app-level keydown handler called `setActive(sessions[slot].characterId)` and stopped there — the DOM toggle (`session-shell--hidden` flipping on the prior tab and off the new one) happens on the next React commit, but no focus call followed it. Focus stayed wherever it was (often nowhere). Fix: added a `refocusActiveCommandBar()` helper that runs `requestAnimationFrame(() => document.querySelector('.session-shell:not(.session-shell--hidden) .command-input')?.focus())` — the rAF waits for React's commit; the selector picks the now-visible session-shell's input naturally because hidden shells are `display:none`. Called from both the Ctrl+1..9 and Ctrl+Tab branches. Not called from the Ctrl+Shift+Enter branch — focus should land in QuickSend, which already auto-focuses its own input. |
| B90 | The floating compass overlay is hard to read — the wrapper background is nearly transparent and inactive directions are almost invisible | v0.7.1 | Reported by the developer. The v0.7.x compass redesign rendered the compass as a semi-opaque card (`background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55)` + 1px white-7%-alpha border + 2px backdrop blur) with each direction as a chip (active fill + border + box-shadow glow). On a black story window the card was nearly invisible AND blurred the text behind it; on a light theme it would have dropped a black blob on the page. Inactive cells were `opacity: 0.18` — too aggressive, the compass shape was unreadable at rest. The fix iterated through several intermediate forms — first a themed `--compass-panel-bg` var with the alpha tuned across multiple rounds (0.82 → 0.62 → 0.40, the developer wanted text behind the card to read sharply), then the backdrop blur removed, then the card itself removed entirely. **Final landing: chrome-less compass.** The wrapper has no background, border, or padding; cells float over the game text. Active cells illuminate via themed text color + `text-shadow` glow (using `--compass-active-glow`) — no chip background, no border. Inactive cells at `opacity: 0.45` so the compass shape is visible at rest. Arrow glyphs got `-webkit-text-stroke: 0.6px currentColor` because Unicode arrows (↖ ↑ ↗ …) barely respond to font-weight — text-stroke thickens them reliably regardless of font. The intermediate `--compass-panel-bg` / `--compass-panel-border` / `--compass-active-bg` / `--compass-active-border` / `--compass-inactive-bg` / `--compass-inactive-border` CSS vars and their Theme Editor entries were stripped after the chrome-less switch (dead config — nothing rendered them). Live themed surface: `--compass-active-text`, `--compass-active-glow`, `--compass-inactive-text`, `--compass-center-text`. Special-exit label `DN` → `DOWN` while we were here. |
| B89 | A connected-but-inactive character tab looks noticeably darker than the active tab | v0.7.0 | Reported by the developer. The base `.character-tab` text color was `--text-dim` — the *de-emphasized* text variable — and only `.character-tab--active` lifted it to full brightness. So a perfectly healthy connected character on an inactive tab read as dim, drifting visually toward the **disconnected** state (which dims via `opacity: 0.55` + italic). Fix: the base `.character-tab` color is now `--text-secondary` (near-full strength). Three intentional brightness tiers result — active (full + bold + background + border), inactive-connected (near-full), disconnected (faded). The active tab is now distinguished by its background/border/weight rather than by dimming its neighbours. One-line CSS change; the bold-weight difference on the active tab is kept as the intended "active" cue. |
| B88 | The map for an inactive character drifts off-centre — tab back and the player is off the side of the map, the view stuck in a corner | v0.7.0 | Reported by the developer running one character while playing another. An inactive character's GameWindow is `display:none`, so its map SVG measures **0×0** — `clientWidth`/`clientHeight` read 0. The follow-camera `useLayoutEffect` correctly bails on `!w||!h`, so the camera transform just goes **stale** while the character travels in the background; `centerOnCurrent` was *unguarded* and could additionally write a **garbage** transform (`x = -cx*scale`). The follow effect only re-fires on the next move, so tabbing back left the camera pointing rooms away (a `look` "fixed" it only by nudging `currentLocation` enough to re-fire the follow once visible). Fix: guarded `centerOnCurrent` against 0 dimensions; a `ResizeObserver` on the SVG recenters on the player when the map regains a non-zero box (tab shown again, or a genuine panel resize). The canonical multi-character map pitfall — hidden ≠ unmounted, but hidden = unmeasurable (CLAUDE.md #24). **Follow-up within v0.7.0:** the first cut wired the `ResizeObserver` in a mount-time `useEffect`, which never attached — GenieMapView's SVG mounts *after* the Genie-loading early-return clears, so at effect time `svgRef.current` was still null (the exact B58 trap). Corrected to wire the observer in the SVG's callback ref (`setSvgRef`), alongside the wheel handler, so it attaches whenever the element actually mounts. |
| B87 | Automapper loses the player's location while running; have to type `look` at the destination for the marker to snap | v0.7.0 | Reported by the developer — intermittent ("doesn't happen every time"). The Genie map disambiguates same-titled rooms (the Crossing has 7 rooms titled "...Moonstone Street") by the room **description** — but while *running*, the game streams room *titles* with no fresh description per step. For an ambiguous title `currentLocation` fell through to file order (`pool[0]`), frequently the wrong same-named room; `look` sent a real description and corrected it. Unique-titled rooms never had the problem — hence the intermittency. Fix: **graph-adjacency disambiguation** — when the title is ambiguous and the description doesn't resolve it, prefer the candidate joined by a Genie arc to the room the player was last in (`prevLocRef`, updated one step behind). You walked here from there, so you must be in a neighbour. Resolution order: description → adjacency → file order. |
| B86 | Genie map causes heavy frame-rate loss while travelling; idling in a dense town (the Crossing) burns ~30% of the frame budget | v0.7.0 | Reported by the developer with DevTools performance traces. The per-room animated effects (shop glints, water ripples, sparkles, heartbeats, …) are hundreds of CSS-animated SVG elements; traces showed Layerize/Paint/Recalculate Style consuming 50–80% of the frame during cross-map runs. The existing `genie-pan-dragging` freeze set `animation-play-state: paused`, which stops an animation advancing but leaves every element **layer-promoted** — the residual layer churn dominated. Fix, three parts: (1) effects are **omitted from the DOM** (not paused) unless `showEffects` = `mapAnimations && !isDragging && !inMotion` — no elements, no layers — re-mounting `MOTION_QUIET_MS` (600ms) after the player stops; (2) **viewport culling** — the effect memos iterate `nearbyNodes` (rooms in the current pan/zoom, `EFFECT_CAP` 30 backstop) instead of the whole zone; (3) node **hover suppressed during motion** (the map scrolling under a stationary cursor was a pointer-event storm). Healer heartbeats kept always-on (priority effect, rare/cheap); player-housing flicker dropped (visual noise). |
| B85 | Connecting via Lich is unreliable — sometimes fails with "Could not connect to Lich" and needs a retry, sometimes works | v0.7.0 | Reported by the developer. `LichConnection.launch()` spawned Lich then waited a **fixed `lichDelay` (5s) timer** before a single connect attempt. Lich's startup time is variable (Ruby init, ~40 `require`s, the listener bind — which itself retries) — slower than 5s and the connect hit `ECONNREFUSED` even though Lich *was* coming up; faster and 5s was wasted. Fix: **connect-with-retry** — `connectWithRetry()` retries the real connection (250ms cadence, ≥30s cap) until Lich's front-end port accepts; the first success IS the session socket (no throwaway probe — verified against Lich `main.rb`: the listener accepts exactly one front-end then closes). `launch()` resolves on the child `spawn` event and watches `exit`/stderr so a Lich that dies on startup fails fast with a real message. Multi-character logins **serialize** their spawn→connect window (`serializeLichLaunch`) so concurrent characters reusing port 11024 can't race or cross-wire; a failed launch kills its Lich so it can't squat the port. |
| B84 | Scroll pinning broken — scrolling up to read history drags the view off-screen when new game text arrives | v0.6.12 | Reported by the developer. Scrolling up to re-read something, then having new text arrive, dragged the read position off the top. Root cause: v0.6.8 added `contain: layout style` to `.text-line` / `.text-line-wrap` as a per-row perf hint. `.text-line-wrap` is the react-virtuoso row — layout containment isolates each row from Virtuoso's own item-measurement / scroll-offset bookkeeping, so Virtuoso couldn't keep the scroll position stable when the list updated while scrolled away from the bottom. Found by elimination: the bug reproduced with smooth scroll OFF, and with smooth off every GameWindow scroll-machine path reduces to its exact v0.6.7 value — the CSS was the only always-active change. Fix: removed both `contain` declarations (DO-NOT-RE-ADD comments left in the CSS; CLAUDE.md pitfall #23). v0.6.12 also removed the story-window smooth-scroll feature entirely — a separate product decision (it was opt-in, marginal, and a magnet for false reports), not the fix for this bug. |
| B83 | Smooth scrolling still lags for moderate commands (e.g. `exp`) — B82's flood threshold of 40 lines was too high | v0.6.11 | Follow-up to B82. The v0.6.10 flood detector cured huge bursts (login dump) but hardcoded the trip threshold at 40 lines, so moderate command output slipped under it and kept smooth-scrolling — on a slow/4K rig that still janked, and Binu ended up disabling smooth scroll entirely. Fix: the threshold is now the per-character **`smoothScrollBurstLimit`** setting (Settings → Smooth Scrolling → **Burst limit**, default **25**, range 5–200; lower = snaps to instant sooner). `GameWindow` reads it live via `floodThresholdRef`. Persists to the character profile via the `AppSettings` blob. The login dump (hundreds of lines) trips flood mode at any setting, so startup protection can't be tuned away — the knob only governs whether *moderate* bursts also fall back to instant. |
| B82 | Smooth scrolling lags hard at startup (slow XML/graphical updates) on a 4K display, easing only after a minute or two | v0.6.10 | Reported by Binu. B80 made smooth scroll opt-out; B82 makes the *opted-in* case actually usable. Root cause: smooth scroll holds the viewport mid-catch-up, so during the login XML flood react-virtuoso continuously mounts/unmounts rows as the animation sweeps — a DOM-mutation storm. Binu's DevTools bottom-up profile was ~74% render pipeline (Recalculate Style 21%, Layerize 19%, Paint 15%); on a 4K panel every re-raster is ~4× cost, so the renderer's main thread couldn't drain `game-event` IPC batches, a backlog built, and it took a minute-plus to clear after the flood ended. Fix: **flood-adaptive** smooth scroll. A detector in the game-event handler accumulates main-line count (`floodCountRef`); past `FLOOD_THRESHOLD = 40` with no `FLOOD_WINDOW_MS = 500` gap, `floodRef` trips and `smoothActive()` returns false → instant scroll → the rendered window parks at the bottom, no sweep. Smooth resumes automatically once the burst subsides. Fixes startup AND heavy combat; no new setting. |
| B81 | Lich Map (image view) default zoom is far too close, and the zoom level isn't retained | v0.6.9 | Reported by Binu. The image-onload centering handler in `MapImageView` hardcoded `scale: 3` every time a map image loaded — so the effective default zoom was 3× and nothing the user did to zoom out survived an image/room change or a relaunch. Fix: the zoom is now persisted per-character to `scopedKey(character, 'lichMapScale')` (debounced 400ms, profile-saved); the centering handler reads the live scale via a `scaleRef` instead of the constant. New first-time default `DEFAULT_LICH_SCALE = 1.5` (~4× more surrounding area than the old 3×). `loadLichScale` clamps the stored value to `[MIN_SCALE, MAX_SCALE]`. |
| B80 | Smooth scrolling / Genie map effects cause performance degradation on some machines | v0.6.9 | Reported by Binu — the v0.6.8 smooth-scroll animation "misbehaved" and the map effects degraded performance on his hardware. Resolution is opt-out toggles rather than a code fix, since the effects work fine on most machines: **Settings → Smooth Scrolling** (new, **off by default**) gates both the story-window smooth scroll AND the Genie map camera glide; **Settings → Genie Map Animations** (new, on by default) freezes all per-room category animations — and the locator ping — via the same `animation-play-state: paused` cascade the map already uses while walking. Both settings are per-character and persist to the character profile. |
| B79 | Manually-typed `;list` / `;listall` produces no visible output in the game window | v0.6.9 | The Lich Scripts panel auto-polls `;listall` every few seconds; `LichBridge.interceptLine` consumed (hid) any line matching the script-list format so the auto-poll didn't spam the game window. But it matched on output format alone — a player typing `;list` themselves had their output silently eaten too. Fix: `LichBridge.pollScriptList()` (the auto-poll entry point) now arms a 4-second window; `interceptLine` consumes a matching line only while that window is live. A response arriving with the window disarmed is a player-typed command and is let through to the game window. The panel still refreshes from both. Per-session (`LichBridge` is per-session), so no cross-character bleed. |
| B78 | Genie Maps "you are here" marker gets stuck on the wrong zone; `look` snaps it to the correct one | v0.6.8 | Reported by the developer crossing the Crossing ↔ Segoltha River boundary — marker stayed in Segoltha while the player was in the Crossing, until a `look` re-emitted the room and it corrected. Root cause in `GenieMapView`'s `currentLocation` resolver: it queried the exact-case `byTitle` index first and **only** fell back to the normalized index when `byTitle` was empty. If `byTitle` returned a *stub* (a cross-zone marker node), the "is it empty?" check passed, the normalized lookup never ran, and the stub — which lives in the *previous* zone — won the match. `look` rescued it only because a fresh room description happened to give the description-tiebreaker a real room to find. Fix: merge `byTitle` ∪ `byNormalized` (dedup by Entry identity) *before* the non-stub filter, so a real room reachable through the normalized form always beats a `byTitle`-only stub. Remaining edge case noted: a title that matches *only* stubs in both indexes still can't resolve from the title alone. |
| B77 | `Home`/`End` keys scroll the story window instead of editing the command box | v0.6.8 | Reported by Binu. The document `keydown` handler claimed `Home`/`End` for story-scroll whenever focus was the command input (the normal play state) — but testers expect text-editing keys to edit text. `End` also appeared to "do nothing" because `scrollToBottom` referenced a `lines` array captured stale (empty) by the once-at-mount listener, so its `scrollToIndex` was permanently guarded out. Fix: when the command input is focused, `Home`/`End` are left native (cursor to start/end of the typed command); `Ctrl+Home`/`Ctrl+End` still reach the story window (Ctrl+Home in a single-line input is identical to plain Home, so nothing native is lost); `PageUp`/`PageDown` are unchanged. `scrollToBottom` switched to `scrollToIndex({ index: 'LAST' })` so it no longer depends on a captured array. |
| B76 | Scrollbar arrow buttons and thumb drag don't trigger pin/unpin in the story window | v0.6.3 | `onWheel` was the only unpin path — but scrollbar arrow-button clicks and thumb-drag dispatch `scroll` events, never `wheel`, so they couldn't unpin. The Virtuoso `handleVirtuosoScrollRef` was deliberately re-pin-only (B36 trade-off, because Virtuoso's own programmatic scrolls were misdetected as user scrolls). After B36 the `suppressUntilRef` gate around programmatic scrolls is now reliable, so we can safely unpin from `onScroll` *outside* the suppression window. Fix: added an unpin branch to `handleVirtuosoScrollRef` — `dist > 40 && pinnedRef.current → false`. Re-pin still triggers on `dist ≤ 10`. The 10/40px deadband prevents flip-flop near the threshold. |
| B75 | PageUp / PageDown / Home / End ignored in the story window when the command input has focus (i.e. almost always during gameplay) | v0.6.3 | Document `onKeyDown` guard required focus to be *neither* the command input *nor* any text field: `if (active !== inputRef.current && !inTextField)`. Since the command input is auto-focused on game-window mount, the guard always evaluated false and scroll keys never fired. This was an over-correction from B19 (which was meant to allow Home/End cursor-keys in automation text-fields). Fix: replaced with `inOtherTextField = inTextField && active !== inputRef.current`; scroll keys now fire from the command input OR from non-text-field focus, suppressed only when *another* text field (e.g. a highlight rule editor) is focused. Preserves B19's automation-text-fields fix. |
| B74 | Clicking "Login" on a disconnected character's toolbar (the post-disconnect button) closes the tab but doesn't surface any login UI | v0.6.2 | Clicking Login on a disconnected tab fired `onDisconnect()` which called `destroySession` + `removeSession`. With one session: the empty-state full LoginScreen rendered automatically — worked fine. With multiple sessions: the tab closed and the user was dropped on whichever other tab was active, with no path to actually re-login the just-closed character. Dead-end UX. Fix: `onDisconnect` in `App.tsx` also calls `setShowAdd(true)` after `removeSession`. If sessions becomes empty, the full LoginScreen appears (showAdd is moot in empty state). If other tabs remain, the Add Character modal opens so the player can re-add this character (or a different one) immediately. |
| B73 | Tab status indicators redesign — single icon slot, priority queue, no width churn, no reconnect glyph | v0.6.2 | Original v0.6.0 design had three always-rendered glyph slots (🩸/⚠/↺) plus skull replacing health % when dead, which (a) felt visually busy, (b) used a misfit `⚠` warning triangle for roundtime, (c) had no surface for stunned, and (d) shifted tab width when health digit count crossed thresholds (e.g. 100% → 9%). Redesigned to one icon slot per tab with priority resolution Dead > Stunned > Bleeding > Roundtime; the top-priority active condition shows, lower-priority conditions still active in-game but not surfaced on the tab. Health % always visible (no more skull-replaces-health) — dead now puts 💀 in the icon slot and leaves health % showing (naturally red at low values). Roundtime icon swapped ⚠ → ⏳ (hourglass with sand) — colored and semantically right for "waiting period." Stunned icon added (💫). Reconnect icon dropped entirely — disconnect is conveyed by tab dimming + italic, which already loudly signals "this is stale" without an extra glyph; reconnect uses the existing toolbar Login button on the active tab. New `stunned: boolean` on `SessionStatus`; GameWindow pushes it via the existing `indicators.stunned` (already parsed). CSS: icon slot has fixed `1.5em` width centered + `visibility: hidden` when no condition active; health % has fixed `4ch` width right-aligned with tabular-nums so digits realign instead of shifting. Net effect: tab width is locked at character add-time and never shifts during play regardless of state changes; only character-name length varies tab width between tabs. |
| B72 | `Ctrl+1`..`Ctrl+9` and `Ctrl+Tab` (tab-switch hotkeys) don't fire when the command bar is focused — i.e. almost always during gameplay | v0.6.2 | App-level keyboard handler had an `if (inField) return` guard that bailed out for any focused `<input>` / `<textarea>`. The justification ("Ctrl+1 is rare but Tab is everywhere") didn't hold up — neither chord has a text-editing meaning, and the entire point of the hotkey is "jump tabs from wherever your hands are." The command bar auto-focuses on connect so the guard effectively disabled the feature during play. Fix: removed the early-return and reworded the comment. `Ctrl+1..9` and `Ctrl+Tab` now fire regardless of focus. `Ctrl+Shift+Enter` (Quick-Send) was already correctly unguarded. |
| B71 | Exp panel default sort direction is ascending on fresh install — most DR players expect descending (high-rank skills first) | v0.6.2 | Reversal of an earlier v0.6.0 default (which was set to ascending to match other clients). User preference is descending on first run; once toggled, the user's choice persists. Fix: `expSortDesc` initializer now returns `true` when no value is stored (`stored === null ? true : stored === 'desc'`). |
| B70 | Tab status glyphs (🩸 bleeding, ⚠ roundtime) appearing/disappearing causes the tab to grow/shrink and shifts every neighbouring tab | v0.6.2 | The glyphs were conditionally rendered, so toggling them changed the tab's flex layout width. Visible jitter during combat as RT pulsed on and off. Fix: all three glyph slots (🩸, ⚠, ↺) are now always rendered with `visibility: hidden` when inactive — slot remains in the layout but the icon doesn't paint. Bleeding is also suppressed when dead (skull communicates enough). New `.character-tab-glyph--hidden` CSS class. |
| B69 | Character names in tab bar display in whatever case the user typed at login, not the server-canonical case | v0.6.2 | LoginScreen captures the typed character name verbatim ("sekmeht" vs "Agan") and that's what the tab bar showed. The DR server sends the canonical case in the `<app char="Sekmeht" .../>` element (parsed as `PlayerInfoEvent`). Fix: new `updateCharacterName(characterId, character)` method on `SessionsContext`; GameWindow's `player-info` handler calls it on arrival. `SessionRecord.character` is updated to canonical case, tab bar re-renders. `characterId` is unaffected because it's case-normalized — all lookups still work. |
| B68 | Window title shows the last-logged-in character even after switching tabs | v0.6.2 | `document.title` was written in two places inside `GameWindow` (the `player-info` event handler and the disconnect status handler), both gated by `isActiveRef`. Only the active tab wrote — but switching tabs didn't *re-trigger* those events, so the title stayed whatever was last written. Fix: title management centralized in `AppShell` via a new `useEffect` keyed on the active session's `character` / `game` / `status.connected` primitives. Re-fires on tab switch and on the active session's status changes. GameWindow and LoginScreen no longer touch `document.title`. |
| B67 | Settings/theme don't reapply when switching between character tabs | v0.6.0 | Each `GameWindow` had its own `settings` / `currentThemeId` state, but the apply-to-DOM effect only fired on those state changes — not on `isActive` flips. Switching from a tab with dark theme to a tab with classic theme left the first tab's CSS visible until the user changed a setting on the new tab. Fix: added `isActive` to the `applyTheme` / `applySettingsToDOM` `useEffect`'s dep array; effect early-returns when `!isActive` so inactive tabs never write the DOM, and re-applies on switch-back. Each tab now genuinely owns its own visual identity. |
| B66 | Auto-copy on text selection fires `writeClipboard` once per mounted tab | v0.6.0 | `GameWindow` attaches a `document.addEventListener('mouseup')` listener on mount for auto-copy. With multiple characters mounted, every listener fires on a single mouseup and each calls `window.api.writeClipboard(text)` with the same selection. Idempotent so no visible breakage, but wasteful and bizarre. Fix: gated by `isActiveRef.current` so only the visible tab owns the auto-copy. |
| B65 | Map options (label mode, Image/Graph view, Z-level filter, legend toggle) don't persist | v0.6.0 | `MapPanel`'s view-mode toggle stored to unnamespaced `lichborne.mapViewMode` (shared across all characters, not in YAML). `MapGraphView`'s `showAllZ` / `zLevels` / `showLegend` were pure in-memory state — lost on close. `mapLabelMode.v2` was character-scoped but never triggered a profile save, so changes only reached YAML if some other event happened to trigger one. Fix: all four are now per-character via `scopedKey(character, ...)`; each change calls `saveProfile()` (new `useProfileSaver` hook). Round-trip via `importCharacterProfile` populates them on next login. |
| B64 | Per-character `localStorage` writes don't trigger `scheduleProfileSave` — settings lost on app crash within 2.5s debounce window | v0.6.0 | Systemic class of bug. Many sites in `GameWindow` / `ExpPanel` / `MapGraphView` wrote to per-character scoped `localStorage` but didn't schedule a profile save. With the v0.6.0 defense-in-depth shutdown save in place, graceful close still caught them — but a kill-from-task-manager or hard crash within the 2.5s debounce window would lose the change. Fix: new `useProfileSaver()` hook (`hooks/useProfileSaver.ts`) returns a stable `saveProfile()` callback bound to the current character's session info; called at every per-character `setItem` site. Crash-resilient even before the graceful-shutdown defense kicks in. The shutdown defense remains as the safety net. |
| B63 | Same-account double-login disconnects existing character with cryptic "Invalid login key" error | v0.6.0 | DragonRealms only allows one active character per SimuCo account. Before the fix, Lichborne let players click + and log in a second character on the same account; the SGE server rejected the new login with "Invalid login key" and (in some timing) kicked the first session. Two-layer fix: (1) pre-flight check in `LoginScreen.handleConnect` refuses with a clear message if any existing session has the same account; (2) defense-in-depth: if the guard misses (race, casing edge), the "Invalid login key" string from the server is translated into the same hint. Documented in BUGS.md Known Limitations. |
| B62 | Closing a disconnected tab triggers a 5-second phantom `gracefulDisconnect` against a dead socket | v0.6.0 | `App.handleCloseTab` called `window.api.disconnect()` unconditionally, including on tabs whose session was already disconnected. Main's handler ran `connection.gracefulDisconnect()` which sent QUIT to a dead socket and waited the full 5s timeout for a disconnect event that already fired. Functionally harmless (the orphan `.then` resolved into a webContents.send with no listener) but held an unnecessary timer. Fix: `handleCloseTab` checks `target.status.connected` before firing the disconnect IPC. |
| B61 | Quick-Send silently drops commands sent to a disconnected character | v0.6.0 | The target dropdown listed every session including disconnected ones with a `(disconnected)` suffix. Selecting one and hitting Send fired `window.api.sendCommand(deadSessionId, cmd)`; main's `getSession` returned undefined and dropped the command with no feedback. Fix: disconnected `<option>`s are now `disabled`; the default-target picker (next character after active) skips disconnected entries; a "No connected characters" placeholder appears when nothing connectable remains. |
| B60 | LoginScreen modal overwrites the active character's `document.title` | v0.6.0 | The Add Character modal mounts `LoginScreen` over existing tabs; its mount-time `useEffect` set `document.title = 'DR [Not connected] | Lichborne v…'` unconditionally, clobbering the active tab's name from the OS taskbar/window-switcher. Fix: added an `isModal` prop to `LoginScreen`; the title write is gated to `!isModal` so only the full-screen empty-state login owns the title. |
| B59 | Reconnecting within an existing tab breaks game-event routing — empty UI even though commands work | v0.6.0 | `SessionsContext.addSession` replaces an existing record by `characterId`, so the React `GameWindow` stays mounted across reconnects (server drop → click + → log same character back in). But GameWindow's three IPC listeners (`onGameEvent`, `onConnectionStatus`, `onRawXml`) were wired in a `useEffect([])` — the closures captured the OLD `session.sessionId` at mount time. Events for the new sessionId silently failed the filter, leaving the tab visually frozen even while commands and connection were live. Fix: introduced `sessionIdRef` that an effect keeps in sync with `session.sessionId`; the three filter closures now read from the ref. Listeners don't re-attach on reconnect (avoids churn) but their identity check always sees the current id. |
| B58 | Graph map view off-center on startup — requires manual click of ◆ to center on current room | v0.6.0 | Root cause: the fit `useEffect` (which centers on the current room when the zone loads) fired when `currentZone`/`showAllZ`/`zLevels` changed, but NOT when the SVG first mounted. Genie loading shows a spinner (early return, no SVG); when Genie finishes, the SVG mounts via `svgCallbackRef` — but none of the fit-effect deps had changed, so the effect never ran and the transform stayed at `{x:0, y:0, scale:1}`. Fix: added `svgReady` state; `svgCallbackRef` sets it `true` on mount and `false` on unmount; `svgReady` added to the fit `useEffect` deps so the effect fires (and centers on the current room) the moment the SVG is available. |
| B57 | `handleTogglePin` missing `scheduleProfileSave` — pinned skills not written to YAML until disconnect | v0.6.0 | Root cause: `handleTogglePin` in `GameWindow` wrote pinned skills to `localStorage` and updated React state but never called `scheduleProfileSave`. Pinned skills persisted only if the user disconnected cleanly or triggered an automations save by opening/closing the automations panel. Fix: added `scheduleProfileSave(session.account, session.character, session.game, session.useLich)` call inside `handleTogglePin`. |
| B56 | `handleFocusChange` missing `scheduleProfileSave` — badging/guild selection not written to YAML until disconnect | v0.6.0 | Root cause: `handleFocusChange` in `GameWindow` wrote the selected guild to `localStorage` and propagated it to `ExpPanel` but never called `scheduleProfileSave`. Changing badging would not persist to `CharacterName.yaml` until some other event (disconnect, automations save, etc.). Fix: added `scheduleProfileSave(session.account, session.character, session.game, session.useLich)` call inside `handleFocusChange`. |
| B55 | Exp panel sort direction defaulted to Z-A instead of A-Z on fresh install | v0.6.0 | Root cause: sort direction read was `localStorage.getItem('lichborne.expSortDesc') !== 'asc'` — when nothing is stored, `null !== 'asc'` evaluates `true`, so the default was descending (Z-A). Fix: changed to `=== 'desc'` so an absent key correctly defaults to `false` (ascending/A-Z), matching other DR clients. |
| B53 | Injuries panel shows "No active wounds." despite having wounds | v0.3.2 | Root cause: wound detection used `height > 0 \|\| width > 0`, but the game sends `height="0" width="0"` on all body-part `<image>` elements — including wounded ones. The actual wound signal is the `name` attribute: `name="Injury1"` (or 2/3) when wounded, `name` matches the part id (e.g. `"leftArm"`) when healthy. Fix: changed detection to `p.name !== id` in both the injured-parts filter and the per-section filter; `woundLevel()` signature updated to `(id, name)` and extracts severity from the trailing digit in "Injury1/2/3" — `name === id` returns 0. |
| B28 | Advanced/Lich settings reset to defaults in new windows | v0.3.2 | Root cause: separate Electron processes cannot share localStorage (LevelDB file lock). The `_shared.yaml` profile is the correct cross-process store, but it was only written by GameWindow on a successful connect — a second instance opening before any connection read only defaults. Fix: `LoginScreen` now debounce-exports the shared profile (1s delay) whenever the `adv` state changes, so the YAML is always current for any other instance that opens concurrently. |
| B42 | Wrayth import wizard shows duplicate "Substitution rules" and "Wrayth strings" rows | v0.3.2 | Root cause: `parseWraythXml` set both `substitutionCount: stringsCount` and `stringsCount` in its return value — `substitutionCount` renders as "Substitution rules" and `stringsCount` renders as "Wrayth strings" in the wizard Step 3 summary, so both rows appeared for the same `<strings>` block data. Fix: removed the `substitutionCount` assignment from the Wrayth parser (`substitutionCount` belongs to Genie/Frostbite substitution files). `countWraythBlock` logic verified correct with synthetic XML. |
| B52 | `boldDepth` stuck high after Lich script outputs unescaped `<`; bleeds into next session | v0.3.2 | Root cause: a Lich script emitting `60 < 65` in `<pushBold/>60 < 65<popBold/>` causes the tokenizer regex `<[^>]*>` to consume ` 65<popBold/>` as a single malformed tag — `<popBold/>` is swallowed and `boldDepth` is never decremented. All subsequent text in the session renders bold (yellow in the dark theme). Fix 1: `boldDepth` now resets to 0 in the `<prompt>` handler — prompts are frame boundaries and bold cannot legitimately survive one. Fix 2: `parser.reset()` added to the `CH.LOGIN` IPC handler so stuck style state cannot carry into a new session. |
| B51 | Empty detail panel renders when current room has no Genie augment | v0.3.1 | Fixed: outer condition was `selectedRoom \|\| selectedOrphan` — when `selectedId` tracks a room not in `augments`, `selectedRoom` is truthy but `selectedRoomAug` is undefined so neither branch renders, leaving an empty bordered div. Fixed: `(selectedRoom && selectedRoomAug) \|\| selectedOrphan`. |
| B50 | Orphan selection leaks into room detail when player moves | v0.3.1 | Fixed: the currentRoom tracking `useEffect` set `selectedId` but never cleared `selectedOrphan`, so both could be truthy simultaneously after moving away from an orphan selection. Fixed: clear `selectedOrphan(null)` alongside `setSelectedId` in the tracking effect. |
| B49 | ◆ Recenter button jumps to wrong coordinates when browsing a different zone | v0.3.1 | Fixed: `recenter()` used `aug.x/y` from the current room's Genie augment, but those coordinates are local to the player's zone — applying them while viewing a different zone jumps to an unrelated position. Fixed: `recenter()` now calls `setCurrentZone(aug.zoneName)` first if the current room's zone differs from the displayed zone, then centers. Button tooltip changes to "Return to my location" when zones differ. |
| B48 | Detail panel stays on selected room after walking somewhere else | v0.3.1 | Fixed: detail panel now tracks `currentRoom` — a `useEffect` on `currentRoom?.id` updates `selectedId` to `currentRoom.id` whenever the player moves, keeping the panel current. Clicking ✕ still dismisses it manually. |
| B47 | Mouse wheel zoom never worked in graph view | v0.3.1 | Fixed: `useEffect([], [])` attached the wheel listener at mount, but the SVG element doesn't exist yet during early-return loading states — so `svgRef.current` was null and `addEventListener` was never called. Fix: replaced the static ref + useEffect pattern with a callback ref (`svgCallbackRef`) that attaches the non-passive wheel listener immediately when the SVG element mounts. |
| B46 | ⊡ Fit button broke mouse wheel zoom | v0.3.1 | Fixed: toolbar buttons (⊡, +, −, ◆, z-chips, ▤, ■) were stealing focus from the SVG canvas on click, causing subsequent wheel events to target the button/toolbar rather than the SVG. Fix: `onMouseDown={e => e.preventDefault()}` on all map control buttons prevents focus transfer while still allowing the click action. |
| B45 | Two ◆ Re-center buttons appeared in graph view | v0.3.1 | Fixed: the ◆ "Re-center on current room" button appeared both in the top toolbar subbar and the bottom navigation bar. Removed the duplicate from the top subbar. |
| B44 | Current room indicator never matched "Abandoned Road" | v0.3.1 | Fixed: subtitle format is `[Room Name - LichID]` with the Lich numeric ID inside the brackets, not `[Name] (SimuID)`. The parser's `idMatch` regex looked for `(\d+)` in parentheses — always returned null for this format. Without an ID, title-only matching failed because multiple rooms share the name "Abandoned Road". Fix: `StormFrontParser` now extracts the trailing `- NNNN` from inside the bracket content (`/\s*-\s*(\d+)\s*$/`) and emits it as `roomId`; `RoomState` stores `roomId`; `MapPanel` does `lichDb.get(roomId)` as the primary lookup with title+desc as fallback. |
| B43 | Genie node for "Bulk Materials" shows as unmatched despite room existing in Lich | v0.3.1 | Fixed: Genie stores short room names ("Bulk Materials") while Lich titles are fully-qualified ("Leth Deriel, Bulk Materials"). The matching pipeline had no step to construct the full title from zone context. Fix: added step 3 to Genie matching — build `"${zone.name}, ${node.name}"` and look it up in the Lich title index, with description disambiguation on multiple hits. Also fixed `lichTitle()` to strip any number of leading/trailing brackets (single or double) so `[[Room Name]]` titles match correctly. |
| B41 | Wrayth client-side commands imported as READY | — | Fixed: `\xxml toggle containers` and similar client commands used the Wrayth `\x` direction prefix, so `isBuiltinAction` was checked before `\x` was stripped — the prefix prevented matching. Fix: strip `\x` first, then check builtin. Also fixed `{BufferTop}`/`{BufferBottom}` — set had `bufftop`/`buffbottom` but Wrayth uses `BufferTop`/`BufferBottom` in braces. |
| B39 | Stream panels permanently stuck in unpinned mode after scrolling up | — | Fixed: `handleScroll` in StreamPanel only set `pinnedRef = false` (on dist > 40) and never set it back to `true`. Once a user scrolled up in any panel — Thoughts, Arrivals, Deaths, custom Lich script panels — auto-follow was broken for the rest of the session. Fix: `pinnedRef.current = dist <= 40` — the scroll handler now re-pins when the user scrolls back within 40px of the bottom. |
| B37 | `<clearStream>` XML tag ignored for custom/Lich script streams | — | Fixed: parser's `clearstream` case only emitted a `clear-stream` event when the stream ID was found in `STREAM_MAP` or `COMPONENT_STREAM` (built-in streams only). Custom stream IDs like `LichScripts` and `moonWindow` were silently dropped. Fix: fall back to the raw `id` when not in either map — consistent with how `pushStream` handles unknown IDs. Scripts that send `<clearStream id="LichScripts"/>` + `<pushStream id="LichScripts"/>` now correctly wipe the panel before writing the new snapshot. |
| B36 | Lines clipped at bottom after command entry | — | Fixed (multiple root causes): (1) Scroll handler's `dist > 80` check was treating Virtuoso's own programmatic scroll events as user scroll-up, clearing pin state — fixed: scroll handler now ONLY re-pins (never un-pins); un-pin is exclusively via `onWheel`/`PageUp`/`Home`. (2) Direct `el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight - el.clientHeight` used DOM `scrollHeight` which only reflects rendered items + spacer estimates; items beyond the current viewport had 0/minimal spacer height so scroll landed far short — fixed: removed direct scrollTop from `useLayoutEffect`; restored Virtuoso's `followOutput` prop which uses Virtuoso's internal height map for ALL items (rendered or not). (3) `totalListHeightChanged` callback provides a fine correction pass after ResizeObserver fires — re-reads `scrollHeight` and forces scroll to true bottom if `dist > 2`. (4) `suppressUnpinRef` (200ms) covers all programmatic scroll events from `followOutput` so they don't spuriously un-pin. (5) `clearLines` was not resetting pin state — user was left stranded at top after clear; fixed: `clearLines` now sets `pinnedRef.current = true`. |
| B38 | Last visible line always clipped by ~1 character height | — | Fixed: `margin-bottom: 0.15em` on `.text-line` collapsed through `.text-line-wrap` (a block container with no padding-bottom or border-bottom) — CSS margin collapsing means this gap was NOT included in Virtuoso's ResizeObserver measurement of item height. The last rendered line was consistently clipped by that margin even when `dist=0`. Fix: removed `margin-bottom` from `.text-line`; moved spacing to `padding-bottom: 0.15em` on `.text-line-wrap` — padding IS captured by ResizeObserver. |
| B35 | Main text area visually inset; compass misaligned; scroll landing ~5 lines short | — | Fixed: `.text-window` had `padding: 8px 12px` which inset the Virtuoso scroller inside the container — scrollbar floated in the padding gutter instead of sitting flush at the edge, and `FloatingCompass` appeared misaligned with the panel border. The left/right padding also reduced item render width relative to what Virtuoso had estimated, causing height estimation errors that made `followOutput` and `scrollToIndex` land short of the true bottom. Fix: removed padding from `.text-window`; applied horizontal spacing per-item via `<div className="text-line-wrap">` wrapper in `itemContent` (`.text-line-wrap { padding: 0 12px; }`). Virtuoso scroller now fills the container flush. |
| B34 | Lich script streams not reaching their pinned panels | — | Root cause: inconsistent case handling — `stream-text` wrote to `streamLines["LichScripts"]` while `stream-declare`/`stream-push` registered the tab as `"lichscripts"` (lowercased), so the panel key never matched. Initial fix lowercased `stream-text`; subsequently all normalization was reversed: stream IDs are now preserved in their original case throughout (`stream-text`, `stream-declare`, `stream-push`, `echoToStream`, `clearstream` parser case, `makeCustomTab`). Consistent raw-case handling means clear/push/display all use the same key the script sends. `NEVER_DISCOVER` check remains case-insensitive (hardcoded lowercase constants). |
| B33 | Main window lag during heavy combat / movement bursts | — | Root cause: 2000 DOM nodes rendered at all times — Chrome DevTools trace showed Layout at 40.9% and removeChild at 29.7% of total frame time. Fix: replaced `lines.map(<TextLineRow>)` with `react-virtuoso` virtual scroller (`<Virtuoso data={lines} itemContent={...}>`). Only ~50 visible rows are in the DOM at any time. Scroll-following via `useLayoutEffect` + `scrollToIndex('LAST')`; un-pinning via direct scroll listener on the Virtuoso scroller element; `suppressUnpinRef` 150ms gate prevents Virtuoso's own programmatic scroll events from spuriously clearing the pin; `overflow-x: hidden` set directly on the scroller element to preserve word wrap. |
| B31 | Stale closure in async Browse button handlers (Highlights + Triggers) | — | Fixed: `await window.api.browseFile(...)` captures `draft`/`action` at click time; if any state updated while the file dialog was open the subsequent setter would overwrite those changes. HighlightsPanel fixed via `setDraft(prev => ...)` functional setter. TriggersPanel fixed via `actionRef.current` pattern — ref is updated on every render so the async handler always spreads the latest action. |
| B32 | Trigger echo stream case-sensitive — "Log" not received by "log" panel | — | Originally fixed by lowercasing all stream IDs at ingestion. Subsequently reversed: stream IDs are now preserved in their original case throughout the system (see B34). Trigger echo stream names in the trigger editor must exactly match the case of the stream/panel ID. Built-in game streams (`log`, `thoughts`, etc.) are always lowercase as the server sends them. |
| B30 | Map panel ignores theme on custom themes created before map theming | Binu | Fixed: `applyCustomTheme` was not merging with `darkBase` before applying; custom themes only set the vars they explicitly define, so newly-added `--map-*` vars were never set and the map fell back to stale/wrong colors. Fix: `applyCustomTheme` now does `{ ...darkBase, ...vars }` before applying — existing custom themes automatically receive correct map defaults without needing to be rebuilt |
| B27 | Map walk sends blank command when arc has empty move | — | Fixed: exit-direction click and BFS walk were passing `arc.move` directly; added `arc.move &&` guard so empty-move arcs (malformed XML) are silently skipped |
| B26 | Active walk timers keep firing after zone switch | — | Fixed: `loadZone` now calls `cancelWalk()` at the top before loading a new file, clearing any in-progress walk timer queue |
| B25 | Indexing progress counter always showed 0 | — | Fixed: `allZonesRef.current.size` is a ref mutation — doesn't trigger re-renders; replaced with `indexedCount` state that increments every 5 files during `buildIndex`; toolbar now shows `indexing… (45/120)` |
| B24 | `computeFit` map off-center when scale is clamped on zone load | — | Fixed: when no current room is known, `fit.x/fit.y` were computed at `fit.scale` then scale was overridden to `Math.max(0.5, fit.scale)` without recomputing offsets; now extracts center point `cx/cy` from the fit result and recomputes `x/y` for the actual scale |
| B23 | `currentRoomId` prop declared and passed but never used | — | Fixed: removed from `MapPanel` interface, `PanelFrame` props/renderPanel signature, and `GameWindow` state; `setCurrentRoomId` call in the `room-title` event handler also removed |
| B22 | `LabelMode` type declared inside component function body | — | Fixed: moved to module scope above the component |
| B29 | Hide Lich Window checkbox has no effect | Binu | Fixed: Electron is a GUI app with no console — direct spawn with `windowsHide: false` produces no visible window because there is no parent console to inherit. Hidden path: direct spawn with `windowsHide: true` (unchanged). Visible path: `shell: true` routes through `cmd.exe` which creates its own visible console for Lich output |
| B21 | Map panel never matches current room | Sekmeht | Fixed: game subtitle `[Room Name - NNNN]` had the Simutronics room number captured as part of the title; stripped with `/\s*-\s*\d+\s*$/` in `StormFrontParser` before emitting `room-title`; additionally the map was rendering upside-down (y-axis negated incorrectly — XML already uses screen-down y convention); both fixed |
| B19 | Home/End keys don't move cursor in automation text fields | Sekmeht | Fixed: `onKeyDown` document listener in GameWindow intercepted Home/End for all elements except the command input, calling `e.preventDefault()` before the focused input could handle it; added `HTMLInputElement`/`HTMLTextAreaElement` guard so scroll-key handling is skipped whenever any text field has focus |
| B18 | Auto-copy on mouse-up no longer works | Binu | Fixed: `setPermissionCheckHandler` added for Local Font Access in v0.1.9 blocked all other permissions, and even with `clipboard-write` whitelisted Electron's internal permission name didn't match, so `navigator.clipboard.writeText` silently failed. Fix: replaced web clipboard API with Electron's native `clipboard.writeText` via IPC (`write-clipboard` channel in main process, `window.api.writeClipboard` in renderer) — no permissions needed, synchronous, reliable. |
| B20 | Scroll pin breaks after ~2000 new lines (combat and swimming) | Binu | Fixed: three layered causes. (1) Pre-`setLines` DOM check unconditionally overwrote `pinnedRef` — fixed to only re-check when already `true`. (2) `handleScroll` re-pinned on `dist < 2` — removed; `handleScroll` now only un-pins, re-pinning is exclusive to `scrollToBottom()`. (3) Root cause: `.text-window` has `overflow-anchor: none`, so trimming lines from the top does not adjust `scrollTop` — the view drifts forward toward newer content with every trim cycle while scrolled up. Fix: don't trim while unpinned; lines append freely when scrolled up; badge/End re-pins and trims to MAX_LINES; hard cap at 3×MAX_LINES (6000 lines) auto-resumes. Tested stable at 3600+ new lines. |
| B17 | Scroll pin stops working during combat | Binu | Fixed: combat sends lines so fast that a second `useLayoutEffect → scrollIntoView` fires before the browser scroll event sets `pinnedRef = false`; added `onWheel` handler on the text window to set `pinnedRef = false` immediately (synchronously, before the DOM scroll) when user wheels up; also fixed `PageUp`/`Home` keyboard handlers which had the same gap |
| B16 | New-lines badge appears but main window still auto-scrolls when scrolled up | Sekmeht | Fixed: `pinnedRef` was set by the async `onScroll` DOM event; if game lines arrived before that event fired, `useLayoutEffect` would see a stale `true` and scroll anyway. Fix: GameWindow re-reads scroll position directly from the DOM right before `setLines`; StreamPanel snapshots scroll position during the render phase (before commit) so the layout effect always has the accurate pre-update pinned state. Note: this fix introduced B20 (trimming race) which was subsequently fixed. |
| B15 | RXP usable time shows dash when pool exceeds one hour | Sekmeht | Fixed: `rexpUsable` regex only matched `minutes`; server sends `Usable This Cycle: 5:56 hours` (hours format) when pool is large; updated to match both formats and include unit in result; ExpBrief mode sends empty `exp rexp` component — RXP row correctly absent |
| F10 | Rank-gain detection and highlight in exp panel | Sekmeht | Fixed: parser detects `<b>` wrapper inside `<component id='exp ...'>` via `CaptureContext.hasBold`; emits `rankUp: true` on `ExpComponentEvent`; `rankUpSkills: Set<string>` state in `GameWindow` with 3s auto-clear timers via `rankUpTimersRef`; skill rows render bold for 3s on rank gain via `exp-row--rank-up` CSS class |
| F11 | Death's Sting indicator in exp panel | Sekmeht | Fixed: the second `exp rexp` component overwrites the first in the same event batch, so `skills['rexp']` becomes the Sting message when active; `ExpFooter` detects via `rexp.startsWith("[Because of Death's Sting")`; red italic "Death's Sting" badge shown in footer; RXP data hidden while active (rested exp is not usable anyway); clears automatically when next batch has only the normal rexp summary |
| B40 | Raw XML tab in Debug panel doesn't lock scroll position when scrolled up | — | Fixed: same root cause as B14 — `rawXmlLines` buffer is trimmed with `shift()` in GameWindow on overflow, but the rawxml tab used `key={i}` (index-based). When items were spliced from the front, all DOM nodes updated their text content, making the view appear to scroll forward. Fixed with `rawBaseRef`/`prevRawLenRef` stable key offset in `DebugPanel` — `key={rawBaseRef.current + i}`, matching the existing `eventBaseRef` pattern from B14. |
| B14 | Events tab in Debug panel doesn't lock scroll position when scrolled up | — | Fixed: index-based `key={i}` caused all 500 DOM nodes to update their text content when items were spliced from the front of the buffer (on overflow), making the view appear to scroll forward even with no programmatic scroll; fixed with `eventBaseRef` stable key offset in `DebugPanel` — `key={eventBaseRef.current + i}` gives each event a globally unique key so React removes trimmed nodes from the top and adds new nodes to the bottom instead of re-using existing DOM nodes |
| B03 | Page Up/Down doesn't scroll main story window | Legiro | Fixed: PageUp/PageDown/Home/End keyboard handlers added to GameWindow; scrollbar arrows added via CSS `::-webkit-scrollbar-button` SVG data URIs |
| B01 | `<a href>` links render as plain text | Legiro, Binu | Fixed: `<a href>` tags parsed in StormFrontParser; `href` property on TextSegment; links render as `.url-link` spans opening in OS browser via `shell.openExternal`; `<LaunchURL src>` also handled; bare http/https URLs in plain text auto-detected with settings toggle (on by default) |
| B04 | Stat column alignment breaks when stats are buffed | Binu | Fixed: `<output class="mono"/>` tag now sets `monoMode` in the parser; lines emitted in mono mode carry `mono: true` on `TextLine`; renderer applies `white-space: pre` to preserve fixed-width column spacing; follow-up: `<preset>` captures inside mono blocks no longer trim leading spaces, preserving column positions for highlighted stats |
| B54 | Mono-mode lines (health, `>exp` output) don't wrap — run off the right edge of the panel | Legiro | Fixed: `TextLineRow` applied `white-space: pre` to lines emitted inside `<output class="mono"/>` blocks; `pre` suppresses all wrapping. Fix: changed to `white-space: pre-wrap` — preserves column spacing and leading whitespace while still wrapping at the panel boundary. |
| B06 | ExpBrief mode breaks exp window | Binu | Fixed: ExpBrief drops mindstate names from component updates, sending only `[x/34]` bracket notation; `parseExp` now falls back to parsing that bracket index when no mindstate string is found |
| B02 | No disconnect reason shown | Legiro | Fixed: on any disconnect (drop, death, QUIT, timeout) the game screen stays open; toolbar button changes to "Login" styled in accent color; Debug panel auto-opens so the Raw XML tab is visible; clicking "Login" returns to the login screen |
| B07 | Inventory list appears in main story window at login | — | Fixed: `<inv id='stow'>item</inv>` container tags were in SILENT_TAGS (suppressing only the tag, not the text between them); now uses a capture context to absorb and discard the content; also silenced `exposecontainer`, `clearcontainer`, `playerid`, and `mode` tags that were generating debug noise |
| B05 | Mana bar should be hidden for NMUs | Binu | Fixed: vitals state initializes empty; bars are only added when the server sends a `vital-update` for them; Thieves receive no mana bar XML so none is shown; Barbarians receive `id='mana'` with `text='inner fire'` so their bar appears with the correct label; when Thief Magic is eventually added by Simutronics the bar will appear automatically |
| F02 | Script-watch status panel | Binu | Resolved: Binu located the correct Lich script (script-watch) that already provides this functionality |
| F04 | Flag toggle buttons | Binu | Resolved: `<d cmd="flag X on/off">` links render as clickable and send the command to the game — no additional work needed |
| F01 | Save login credentials | Legiro | Resolved: account name persists via `localStorage` (`lichborne.account` key); password intentionally blank on every launch |
| B08 | Horizontal scrollbar appears in main text window; text doesn't word-wrap | — | Fixed: `overflow-y: auto` on `.text-window` implicitly set `overflow-x: auto`; added `overflow-x: hidden` to force word-wrap at window boundary |
| B11 | Debug button steals focus at startup; Debug panel auto-opens on intentional disconnect | Legiro | Fixed: `autoFocus` on command input ensures it wins focus on game screen load; clean disconnect tracked via `cleanDisconnect` flag in main process — debug panel no longer auto-opens on QUIT, exit command, or Disconnect button |
| B12 | Raw XML tab not scrolled to bottom when first opened | Binu | Fixed: `useEffect` on tab change scrolls to bottom when switching tabs; Raw XML now lands at latest content on first open |
| F06 | Copy All button in debug panel | Sekmeht | Fixed: Copy All button in debug toolbar and right-click context menu; events tab copies one JSON object per line, Raw XML tab copies raw lines |
| F09 | Debug streams run unconditionally in background | Sekmeht | Fixed: `showDebugRef` gates both `setDebugEvents` and `setRawXmlLines`; no re-renders from debug collection while panel is closed |
| B09 | Scrolling up doesn't lock scroll position | Binu | Closed — tested and working as expected; scroll pins correctly when scrolling up, resumes on End/scroll-to-bottom |
| B10 | Highlight list swatch shows wrong color for Match scope | Legiro | Fixed: `listItemSwatch()` now prefers bgColor for both Line and Match scope, falling back to textColor if no bg set |
| B13 | Scrollbar shows text cursor instead of pointer | Binu | Fixed: `cursor: default` added to `::-webkit-scrollbar-track` and `::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb` in global.css |
| F08 | Command history deduplication | Binu | Fixed: consecutive duplicate commands skipped from history; spamming the same command stores only one entry |
